# UI and interaction parity

> **Last reviewed:** 2026-10-10 · **Last updated:** 2026-10-26 · **Change:** minor (Selection tool: a double-click on a threaded port breaks the thread, `text.thread.break`; previously:; Pathfinder Options; previously:; Window › Brush Libraries and the Brushes panel's Brush Libraries Menu open brush library panels; brushes click to add (and apply to the selected paths), drag onto a path or the Brushes panel; previously: Delete Empty Artboards; previously: a selected Grid Repeat shows handles: the one below the grid adds or removes rows, the one right of it columns, the one past its bottom-right corner both, and the sliders between the first two columns and rows set the horizontal and vertical spacing; each drag is one undo step and Escape cancels it (#2691); scores and estimates unchanged; previously:; Transform panel menu: Transform Object Only / Pattern Only / Both and Use Registration Point for Symbol; previously:; General › Corner Radius acts for the Rounded Rectangle tool (#394); previously:; Pencil Fidelity down to 0.01 pt on a logarithmic slider with fitting key steps (#3328); scores and estimates unchanged) · **Target:** Adobe Illustrator 2026 (30.x; bezier drag previews follow the pointer back into the window and to the release, #834; Guides: a path moved by a press on its stroke or beside its edge, and a straight segment Direct Selection drags, snap by the nearest point of the path, landing exactly on a ruler guide (#3701); scores and estimates unchanged; previously:; Artboard Options honors Scale Artwork with Artboard for preset, orientation and typed-size changes; both Fits keep artwork stationary; scores and estimates unchanged; Tangent and straight-segment perpendicular snapping use saved Smart Guides Geometric Guides preferences and Control bar quick access (#2537); no View submenu; G13: gradient Method (Illustrator 30.0): a Method menu in the Gradient panel, Classic or Perceptual (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `method`, saved in the native file only when Perceptual, so older files open as Classic); Perceptual mixes a linear or radial gradient's colours in OKLab, on the canvas and in raster exports, and SVG and PDF exports write it as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes; Classic draws as before; a dithered Perceptual fill takes dither's noise; score and hours unchanged; G13: gradient Dither (Illustrator 30.0): a Dither box in the Gradient panel (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `dither`, saved in the native file) draws a linear or radial gradient fill with seeded fine noise that hides banding, on the canvas and in raster exports; SVG and PDF exports write the fill as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes dither; fills only, as documented; score and hours unchanged; Recolor Artwork Edit chips scroll within the available screen height (#3416); small palettes retain their tab height; scores and estimates unchanged; Blend panel Type and Ramp control step easing with a separate color Shift (#2539); Blend Options retains spacing and orientation; polynomial curves are original approximations; Panels dock into any dock group or the icon column by dragging (UI.6), workspaces keep their layout changes (UI.7), dock groups collapse to their tabs by a double-click (UI.8); scores unchanged; Shape Builder Gap Detection shares Live Paint’s spatial gap finder and temporary merge bridges (#2526); Custom is capped at 72 pt in the existing options dialog; 3D: original Standard, Diffuse, Top Left and Right lighting presets; command access, effective-setting recognition and preview/history; scores and estimates unchanged; one selected clip group's Properties panel has Edit Clipping Path and Edit Contents at its top, G1; Distribute Objects and Distribute Spacing space from a key guide (#3165) with the Distribute Spacing value; Distribute spaces selected ruler guides with the objects, #1597 in part; the selection tools take a clipping path on its outline, over the art it clips; Polygon and Star exact-size placement remembers the chosen count for later drags and dialog openings; Cancel preserves the previous count; Live trace result bounds and selection highlights exclude the retained bitmap except in source-image views; a ruler guide can be the key object Align aligns to, and selected guides align to a key object, #2143; Pencil: Alt key toggles to Smooth Tool, #2001; status-readout sample widths cached by language, document unit and pixels per point; status messages and readouts use stable responsive allocations sized for localized labels and ordinary coordinates; cached canvas dimensions match the renderer limit before cache-key creation, view construction and worker submission; cached canvas reprojection preserves scale during resize, rotation and DPI changes; on-canvas Mirror Repeat axis, #2877; Background Export progress dialog with Cancel, #2564; 3D lighting: one to ten colored lights, smooth colored Inflate, legacy white-light appearance preserved; surface batching and restored seam minimum; scores and estimates unchanged; Blend spine Anchor Point conversions, selection/stacking priority and Shift regression coverage (#2544); scores and hours unchanged; Artboard Options' X and Y with a reference point, #1876; scores unchanged; library panels: one per library, dockable and tabbable, with Persistent, #2603; the Selection tool threads text from the out port, #2572 in part; scores unchanged; Group Selection picks and moves one path of a compound path, #2441 in part; the Move dialog opens on the last move and its last checkboxes; scores unchanged; transparency grid visibility saved in native documents, #1968; Live Corners radius up to the neighbouring cuts, #2332; Image Trace quick presets: wording corrected to the shipped behaviour, the Image Trace button opens the controls; batch exporting, #1935; document window scroll bars, #2322; Anchor Point tool: a click on a handle end retracts that handle only, #2267; scores unchanged; Smart Guides: the dragged end of a straight segment keeps to its extension at any angle, #2120; Warp Options controls verified by focused checks and original-image review; estimates unchanged; existing-guide Escape cancellation validated 2026-10-14; scores and hours unchanged; open-path anchor Delete keeps surviving runs separate; estimates unchanged; Artboard tab-switch follow-up historically validated on `a0671777`; scores and hours unchanged; Artboard clean active-tab close/Delete follow-up validated 2026-10-15; Curvature curve geometry, #1236 in part; Width-point finite-arithmetic follow-up validated; scores and hours unchanged; Image Trace adjustment discovery; later 2026-10-14 merges: macOS save sheet Format menu back to missing, #1855, #866 reopened; anchor X/Y/W/H in the Transform panel, Properties and Control bar, #1824; Snap to Grid over Snap to Pixel, #1816; toolbar labels, columns and the All Tools drawer, #1751; Actions panel selection, #1629 in part; scores unchanged; 2026-10-14 merges: anchor Align and Distribute, #1636; Delete on closed paths and whole objects, #1674; selection inside isolation, #1666; Escape in a Direct Selection drag and while typing, #1743, #1686; Pen clicks under 3 px, #1663; [ and ] on more tools, #1661 in part; fields commit on click away, #1787; command search keys, #1757; a guide's position in the Transform panel is a convenience with no parity claim, #1659; scores unchanged; intersect Smart Guides for drawn points and dragged anchors, #1602 in part; a ruler guide's position in the Transform panel, #1597 in part; Symbol Options before New Symbol, #1560; tool flyout widths, #1616; Pathfinder Trim, Merge and Crop strokes, #1570; scores unchanged; G1/M11: Artboard deletion and history selection normalization; history regressions and full workspace CI pass; scores and hours unchanged; Width Point Edit: Shift-double-click enables adjoining adjustment; plain double-click keeps it off; scores and hours unchanged; per-corner radius fields with a link toggle, split from #1594; Join merges anchors lying on one another, #1533; correction: Average and Join with a corner or smooth choice is missing, not done; guide objects take the guide colour and style and Lock Guides, #1534; Keyboard Shortcuts lists commands outside the menus, #1539; round corners next to curves and the Smooth tool's reach, #1512; Make Compound Path on a group, #1537; dialog slider rails, #1483; template layers stay in Preview in Outline view, #1486; Direct Selection takes the selected object's anchor where anchors coincide, #1508; Tabs panel stop drags, #1458; Split Into Grid dialog, #1462; Transform Objects and Reflect's angle, #1448 and #1460; Pathfinder panel on a group, #1450; value fields: readable selection, #1465 in part, and a focused field follows its value, #1457; scores unchanged; Transform dialogs have a live Preview and step with the arrow keys, #1412; transform tools, dialogs and Move act on direct-selected anchors, #1408; the bounding box hides during a corner drag, #1229; scores unchanged; dialogs focus their first field on every open, #1367, and a first field that starts greyed out once it is enabled, #1395; scores unchanged; Rectangular Grid and Polar Grid click dialogs take the skew, frame, compound path and Fill Grid options, #1377 in part, score unchanged; Live Corners: a Direct Selection click on a widget selects its corner, #1239; custom ruler origin, #1212; Artboard tool clicks fit the topmost editable object in one undo step (#1227); scores unchanged; Pathfinder single-result source names and SVG IDs retained, #1293; DXF control-point SPLINE closure read, #1299 follow-up; previously:; Artboard Options: Show Video Safe Areas (`showVideoSafeAreas`) outlines the action-safe and title-safe areas 3.5% and 5% in from each edge (EBU R 95) on the canvas only, and Video Ruler Pixel Aspect Ratio (`videoRulerPixelAspectRatio`, 0.1–10, default 1) sets how wide a video pixel is, per artboard, in one undo step and in the native format; View › Rulers › Show Video Rulers (`view.videoRulers`) counts the active artboard in those pixels from its top-left corner (#2849); scores and estimates unchanged; a double-click on the Line Segment, Arc, Rectangular Grid or Polar Grid tool opens its Tool Options, whose OK sets the next drag's and click's options (#2691, in part); Simplify of part of a path: with anchors or segments of a path direct-selected, Object › Path › Simplify (slider, Auto-Simplify and dialog) refits only each run of selected segments between its end anchors and leaves the rest of the path exactly as it was; the Simplify row is done; scores and estimates unchanged; Fix #3705: in Draw Inside, art drawn or pasted inside the isolated object takes isolation mode into its clip group, so it can be clicked and isn't dimmed; undo or redo that removes the isolated object ends isolation mode; Fix #3705 in part: drawing modes apply to pastes (Draw Inside puts Paste, Paste in Place and Paste on All Artboards inside the object drawn into; Draw Behind puts Paste in Place and Paste on All Artboards behind the selection), and Shift+D goes from Draw Behind back to Draw Normal when Draw Inside can't draw into the selection instead of stopping at Draw Behind; previously: Recolor Artwork › Assign: double-click a new color for the Color Picker, which keeps a CMYK mix as typed (part of #4028; Lab can't be typed yet); scores and estimates unchanged; previously: docs catch-up: Relative rounding done (#3091), stale Video rulers duplicate removed; scores and estimates unchanged; previously: Select › Start/Stop Global Edit with the Global Edit Options in Properties › Quick Actions, Escape stops it, #3521; previously:; Window › Symbol Libraries and the Symbols panel's Symbol Libraries Menu open symbol library panels; symbols click to add and drag onto the canvas; previously:; Selection tool: in and out ports on every selected area type and type on a path, the in port threading a new or clicked frame before it, #2572; scores unchanged; Smart guides: Distance Guides, Alt/Option over another object or an empty part of an artboard with the Selection tool, and the Invoke Distance Guides preference; scores and estimates unchanged; previously:; Artboard Options: Show Video Safe Areas (`showVideoSafeAreas`) outlines the action-safe and title-safe areas 3.5% and 5% in from each edge (EBU R 95) on the canvas only, and Video Ruler Pixel Aspect Ratio (`videoRulerPixelAspectRatio`, 0.1–10, default 1) sets how wide a video pixel is, per artboard, in one undo step and in the native format; View › Rulers › Show Video Rulers (`view.videoRulers`) counts the active artboard in those pixels from its top-left corner (#2849); scores and estimates unchanged; a double-click on the Line Segment, Arc, Rectangular Grid or Polar Grid tool opens its Tool Options, whose OK sets the next drag's and click's options (#2691, in part); scores and estimates unchanged; Pencil: Shift held at the press draws a straight line at 45° steps from the Constrain Angle (part of #3799); status-bar pointer readout follow-up; scores and hours unchanged; Simplify of part of a path: with anchors or segments of a path direct-selected, Object › Path › Simplify (slider, Auto-Simplify and dialog) refits only each run of selected segments between its end anchors and leaves the rest of the path exactly as it was; the Simplify row is done; scores and estimates unchanged; Fix #3705: in Draw Inside, art drawn or pasted inside the isolated object takes isolation mode into its clip group, so it can be clicked and isn't dimmed; undo or redo that removes the isolated object ends isolation mode; Fix #3705 in part: drawing modes apply to pastes (Draw Inside puts Paste, Paste in Place and Paste on All Artboards inside the object drawn into; Draw Behind puts Paste in Place and Paste on All Artboards behind the selection), and Shift+D goes from Draw Behind back to Draw Normal when Draw Inside can't draw into the selection instead of stopping at Draw Behind; Tangent and straight-segment perpendicular snapping use saved Smart Guides Geometric Guides preferences and Control bar quick access (#2537); no View submenu; G13: gradient Method (Illustrator 30.0): a Method menu in the Gradient panel, Classic or Perceptual (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `method`, saved in the native file only when Perceptual, so older files open as Classic); Perceptual mixes a linear or radial gradient's colours in OKLab, on the canvas and in raster exports, and SVG and PDF exports write it as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes; Classic draws as before; a dithered Perceptual fill takes dither's noise; score and hours unchanged; G13: gradient Dither (Illustrator 30.0): a Dither box in the Gradient panel (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `dither`, saved in the native file) draws a linear or radial gradient fill with seeded fine noise that hides banding, on the canvas and in raster exports; SVG and PDF exports write the fill as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes dither; fills only, as documented; score and hours unchanged; Recolor Artwork Edit chips scroll within the available screen height (#3416); small palettes retain their tab height; scores and estimates unchanged; Blend panel Type and Ramp control step easing with a separate color Shift (#2539); Blend Options retains spacing and orientation; polynomial curves are original approximations; Panels dock into any dock group or the icon column by dragging (UI.6), workspaces keep their layout changes (UI.7), dock groups collapse to their tabs by a double-click (UI.8); scores unchanged; Shape Builder Gap Detection shares Live Paint’s spatial gap finder and temporary merge bridges (#2526); Custom is capped at 72 pt in the existing options dialog; 3D: original Standard, Diffuse, Top Left and Right lighting presets; Perceptual mixes a linear or radial gradient's colours in OKLab, on the canvas and in raster exports, and SVG and PDF exports write it as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes; Classic draws as before; a dithered Perceptual fill takes dither's noise; score and hours unchanged; G13: gradient Dither (Illustrator 30.0): a Dither box in the Gradient panel (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `dither`, saved in the native file) draws a linear or radial gradient fill with seeded fine noise that hides banding, on the canvas and in raster exports; SVG and PDF exports write the fill as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes dither; fills only, as documented; score and hours unchanged; Recolor Artwork Edit chips scroll within the available screen height (#3416); earlier: Live Corners round corners between curved sides, #1144) · **Target:** Adobe Illustrator 2026 (30.x; floating panels open in OS windows of their own on the desktop (inside the window on the web and on Wayland), and saved layouts from earlier versions convert to the shared docking layout (#4135); scores and estimates unchanged; previously: Recolor Artwork › Assign: double-click a new color for the Color Picker, which keeps a CMYK mix as typed (part of #4028; Lab can't be typed yet); scores and estimates unchanged; previously: docs catch-up: Relative rounding done (#3091), stale Video rulers duplicate removed; scores and estimates unchanged; previously: Select › Start/Stop Global Edit with the Global Edit Options in Properties › Quick Actions, Escape stops it, #3521; previously:; Window › Symbol Libraries and the Symbols panel's Symbol Libraries Menu open symbol library panels; symbols click to add and drag onto the canvas; previously:; Selection tool: in and out ports on every selected area type and type on a path, the in port threading a new or clicked frame before it, #2572; scores unchanged; Smart guides: Distance Guides, Alt/Option over another object or an empty part of an artboard with the Selection tool, and the Invoke Distance Guides preference; scores and estimates unchanged; previously:; Artboard Options: Show Video Safe Areas (`showVideoSafeAreas`) outlines the action-safe and title-safe areas 3.5% and 5% in from each edge (EBU R 95) on the canvas only, and Video Ruler Pixel Aspect Ratio (`videoRulerPixelAspectRatio`, 0.1–10, default 1) sets how wide a video pixel is, per artboard, in one undo step and in the native format; View › Rulers › Show Video Rulers (`view.videoRulers`) counts the active artboard in those pixels from its top-left corner (#2849); scores and estimates unchanged; a double-click on the Line Segment, Arc, Rectangular Grid or Polar Grid tool opens its Tool Options, whose OK sets the next drag's and click's options (#2691, in part); scores and estimates unchanged; Pencil: Shift held at the press draws a straight line at 45° steps from the Constrain Angle (part of #3799); status-bar pointer readout follow-up; scores and hours unchanged; Simplify of part of a path: with anchors or segments of a path direct-selected, Object › Path › Simplify (slider, Auto-Simplify and dialog) refits only each run of selected segments between its end anchors and leaves the rest of the path exactly as it was; the Simplify row is done; scores and estimates unchanged; Fix #3705: in Draw Inside, art drawn or pasted inside the isolated object takes isolation mode into its clip group, so it can be clicked and isn't dimmed; undo or redo that removes the isolated object ends isolation mode; Fix #3705 in part: drawing modes apply to pastes (Draw Inside puts Paste, Paste in Place and Paste on All Artboards inside the object drawn into; Draw Behind puts Paste in Place and Paste on All Artboards behind the selection), and Shift+D goes from Draw Behind back to Draw Normal when Draw Inside can't draw into the selection instead of stopping at Draw Behind; Tangent and straight-segment perpendicular snapping use saved Smart Guides Geometric Guides preferences and Control bar quick access (#2537); no View submenu; G13: gradient Method (Illustrator 30.0): a Method menu in the Gradient panel, Classic or Perceptual (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `method`, saved in the native file only when Perceptual, so older files open as Classic); Perceptual mixes a linear or radial gradient's colours in OKLab, on the canvas and in raster exports, and SVG and PDF exports write it as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes; Classic draws as before; a dithered Perceptual fill takes dither's noise; score and hours unchanged; G13: gradient Dither (Illustrator 30.0): a Dither box in the Gradient panel (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `dither`, saved in the native file) draws a linear or radial gradient fill with seeded fine noise that hides banding, on the canvas and in raster exports; SVG and PDF exports write the fill as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes dither; fills only, as documented; score and hours unchanged; Recolor Artwork Edit chips scroll within the available screen height (#3416); small palettes retain their tab height; scores and estimates unchanged; Blend panel Type and Ramp control step easing with a separate color Shift (#2539); Blend Options retains spacing and orientation; polynomial curves are original approximations; Panels dock into any dock group or the icon column by dragging (UI.6), workspaces keep their layout changes (UI.7), dock groups collapse to their tabs by a double-click (UI.8); scores unchanged; Shape Builder Gap Detection shares Live Paint’s spatial gap finder and temporary merge bridges (#2526); Custom is capped at 72 pt in the existing options dialog; 3D: original Standard, Diffuse, Top Left and Right lighting presets; command access, effective-setting recognition and preview/history; scores and estimates unchanged; one selected clip group's Properties panel has Edit Clipping Path and Edit Contents at its top, G1; Distribute Objects and Distribute Spacing space from a key guide (#3165) with the Distribute Spacing value; Distribute spaces selected ruler guides with the objects, #1597 in part; the selection tools take a clipping path on its outline, over the art it clips; Polygon and Star exact-size placement remembers the chosen count for later drags and dialog openings; Cancel preserves the previous count; Live trace result bounds and selection highlights exclude the retained bitmap except in source-image views; a ruler guide can be the key object Align aligns to, and selected guides align to a key object, #2143; Pencil: Alt key toggles to Smooth Tool, #2001; status-readout sample widths cached by language, document unit and pixels per point; status messages and readouts use stable responsive allocations sized for localized labels and ordinary coordinates; cached canvas dimensions match the renderer limit before cache-key creation, view construction and worker submission; cached canvas reprojection preserves scale during resize, rotation and DPI changes; on-canvas Mirror Repeat axis, #2877; Background Export progress dialog with Cancel, #2564; 3D lighting: one to ten colored lights, smooth colored Inflate, legacy white-light appearance preserved; surface batching and restored seam minimum; scores and estimates unchanged; previously:; Artboard Options honors Scale Artwork with Artboard for preset, orientation and typed-size changes; both Fits keep artwork stationary; scores and estimates unchanged; Tangent and straight-segment perpendicular snapping use saved Smart Guides Geometric Guides preferences and Control bar quick access (#2537); no View submenu; G13: gradient Method (Illustrator 30.0): a Method menu in the Gradient panel, Classic or Perceptual (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `method`, saved in the native file only when Perceptual, so older files open as Classic); Perceptual mixes a linear or radial gradient's colours in OKLab, on the canvas and in raster exports, and SVG and PDF exports write it as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes; Classic draws as before; a dithered Perceptual fill takes dither's noise; score and hours unchanged; G13: gradient Dither (Illustrator 30.0): a Dither box in the Gradient panel (`paint.editGradient`/`paint.setFill` `dither`, saved in the native file) draws a linear or radial gradient fill with seeded fine noise that hides banding, on the canvas and in raster exports; SVG and PDF exports write the fill as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes dither; fills only, as documented; score and hours unchanged; Recolor Artwork Edit chips scroll within the available screen height (#3416); small palettes retain their tab height; scores and estimates unchanged; Blend panel Type and Ramp control step easing with a separate color Shift (#2539); Blend Options retains spacing and orientation; polynomial curves are original approximations; Panels dock into any dock group or the icon column by dragging (UI.6), workspaces keep their layout changes (UI.7), dock groups collapse to their tabs by a double-click (UI.8); scores unchanged; Shape Builder Gap Detection shares Live Paint’s spatial gap finder and temporary merge bridges (#2526); Custom is capped at 72 pt in the existing options dialog; 3D: original Standard, Diffuse, Top Left and Right lighting presets; Distribute spaces selected ruler guides with the objects, #1597 in part; the selection tools take a clipping path on its outline, over the art it clips; Polygon and Star exact-size placement remembers the chosen count for later drags and dialog openings; ## Feature submission evidence

**Feature evidence last updated:** 2026-10-22.

The shared assessment status line and revision history are current upstream; this dated section records the submission's feature evidence and revisions without rescoring upstream or claiming a new combined build or runtime measurement.

- Current-main conflict reconciliation retains upstream and feature behavior; scores and estimates unchanged
- Superseded 2026-10-20 behavior, retained as history: Artboard Options shares existing presets and orientation controls and can fit artwork without moving it; artwork fitting uses the original target artboard and revision-scoped bounds caches

- Artboard Options puts Fit to Artwork Bounds and Fit to Selected Art in the Preset menu. Artwork bounds cover all drawn document art, including other artboards and off-board art; selection bounds may span artboards. Fit changes only the active artboard and keeps artwork stationary, including position-only fitting with Move Artwork enabled. Existing Background Color and presets remain available; scores and estimates unchanged.

Artboard Options forwards the active Artboard tool's Scale Artwork with Artboard option for ordinary resizing. Fit to Artwork Bounds and Fit to Selected Art explicitly leave artwork stationary. Dialog regression coverage checks preset/orientation/typed-size intent with scaling on and off, draft cancellation, one-step Undo/Redo, and fitting while Move Artwork and Scale Artwork are enabled. This is a narrow follow-up to #3277; no parity score or estimate is reassessed.

| 2026-10-22 | minor | Reconcile upstream documentation after review F-3; preserve every dated revision and the original scaleArt runtime evidence without repeating completed jobs. |

Existing Background Color and presets remain available; scores and estimates unchanged. |

How VectorCraft's tools, handles, snapping and panels behave next to Illustrator's. A vector illustrator's
trust is in the details: where a handle lands, what Shift and Alt do mid-drag, what a smart guide snaps to,
how far an arrow key nudges. This checklist is the work list for the **interaction-fidelity pass**
([gaps.md](gaps.md) G1). Part of [target-app-parity.md](target-app-parity.md).

**How it is measured:** from the tool state machines' own documentation and tests (`crates/tools/src/*.rs`,
`crates/ui-egui/src/canvas.rs`, `shortcuts.rs`, the `tests_*` files) against Illustrator's public documentation
(helpx tool pages and the keyboard-shortcut reference). Never against the running app: the clean-room rule
forbids launching or measuring Illustrator, so there has been **no side-by-side session**. Every "done" below is
"behaves as the documentation describes", which is weaker than "a power user can't tell". Status:
**done** (documented behaviour implemented and tested), **partial**, **missing**.

## Summary

| Area | % | Remaining (h) | Kind |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Pen, Curvature and anchor tools | 85% | 6–10 | estimated |
| Direct Selection, handles and anchors | 85% | 5–8 | estimated |
| Smart guides and snapping | 70% | 8–12 | estimated |
| Transform handles, bounding box, Free Transform | 80% | 5–8 | estimated |
| Nudging, numeric entry, precision, units | 85% | 3–5 | estimated |
| Modifier keys and temporary tools | 80% | 5–8 | estimated |
| Keyboard shortcuts (default set) | 70% | 6–10 | estimated |
| Cursors | 75% | 3–5 | estimated |
| Panels, Control bar, Properties, context menus | 70% | 15–25 | estimated |
| Look and feel (layout, theme, metrics) | 80% | 5–10 | measured once against public screenshots on 2026-10-02; panels added since not re-measured |
| Accessibility (screen reader, keyboard-only canvas) | 20% | 15–25 | estimated: AccessKit on by default for egui widgets; canvas and custom panels not described, never tested with a screen reader |
| **UI/UX overall** | **~55%** | **~70–110** (overlaps the feature areas) | estimated |

The overall figure is lower than the rows because what users notice first (small differences across many
tools, never checked side by side) isn't captured row by row.

| Cmd/Ctrl lends the last-used selection tool | done | the path being drawn continues afterwards |

| Rubber Band preview | done | Preferences option |
| Auto Add/Delete on a selected path; Shift or the preference disables it | done | |

| Node-editing cursors: Direct Selection's white arrow with a hollow or filled square over an anchor (path unselected or selected); the Reshape Segment cursor over a curved segment (Direct Selection, Anchor Point, the Pen with Alt); the Anchor Point caret for the Pen with Alt; Group Selection's plus (also Direct Selection with Alt); a filled arrow while dragging that turns hollow on a snap; a double arrowhead for an Alt-drag copy | partial | as the public manual describes them, drawn in code; no cue over handles (none documented) |

| Blend spine Anchor Point conversion and handle editing (#2544) | implemented | symmetric handles, independent drag/retraction, visible-handle policy and selection/stacking covered by tool and engine regressions; Windows native canvas checks and screenshots reviewed in PR #2643 |
| Add/Delete/Anchor Point tools; Anchor Point tool drags segments to reshape | done | #2267: a click on a handle end retracts that handle and leaves the anchor a corner with the other one; Shift held at the press on an anchor moves it, handles and all (Shift kept down: at 45° steps) instead of pulling out handles |
| Pencil: Fidelity, Keep selected, Alt to Smooth, edit selected paths, close by proximity | done | Fidelity goes down to 0.01 pt in the dialog, the tool and `path.freehand` (the Smooth tool keeps 0.05); its slider runs across the Tool Options row on a logarithmic rail from 0.01 to 20 pt, the field shows hundredths, and the slider's and the field's arrow keys step hundredths up to 0.1 pt, tenths up to 1 pt and whole points above (Shift ×10) (#3328); Alt key toggles to Smooth Tool (#2001); Shift held at the press draws a straight line at 45° steps from the Constrain Angle, and Alt held at the press (with Alt key toggles to Smooth Tool off) a straight line at any angle (#3799), with a straight-line cursor while Shift or Alt is held before the press, and a path-continuation cursor over an end of a selected open path, where the line continues the path; Edit Selected Paths continues a selected path from an end and, from a stroke that starts and ends on a selected path, redraws the section between (the shorter way round a closed path, #2850); with Keep selected (Pencil and Paintbrush, on by default) off, the path a stroke draws or continues is not selected afterwards; a filled Pencil path keeps a stroke of None (#1804, part of #1679); while a freehand stroke is drawn back to within Close Paths When Ends Are Within of its start, the Pencil and the Paintbrush show the closing cursor, and a release there closes the path |
| Pencil: Fidelity, Keep selected, Alt to Smooth, edit selected paths, close by proximity | done | Alt key toggles to Smooth Tool (#2001); Shift held at the press draws a straight line at 45° steps from the Constrain Angle, and Alt held at the press (with Alt key toggles to Smooth Tool off) a straight line at any angle (#3799), with a straight-line cursor while Shift or Alt is held before the press, and a path-continuation cursor over an end of a selected open path, where the line continues the path; Edit Selected Paths continues a selected path from an end and, from a stroke that starts and ends on a selected path, redraws the section between (the shorter way round a closed path, #2850); with Keep selected (Pencil and Paintbrush, on by default) off, the path a stroke draws or continues is not selected afterwards; a filled Pencil path keeps a stroke of None (#1804, part of #1679); while a freehand stroke is drawn back to within Close Paths When Ends Are Within of its start, the Pencil and the Paintbrush show the closing cursor, and a release there closes the path; a Pencil stroke from an end of one selected open path to an end of another connects the two into one path |

## Keyboard shortcuts

Cmd/Ctrl+/ (`appearance.addFill`) and Cmd/Ctrl+Alt+/ (`appearance.addStroke`) add a new fill and a new stroke to the
selected objects, as the Appearance panel's Add New Fill and Add New Stroke buttons do.

~110 distinct modified shortcuts on menu commands plus single-key tool shortcuts for the default toolbar
(`crates/tools/src/catalog.rs`), a shortcut editor with chords, and saved sets. Cmd/Ctrl+Alt+L (`ui.newLayer`) opens Layer Options for a new layer, as an Alt-click on the Layers panel's New Layer button does, and Cmd/Ctrl+L still adds a layer without the dialog. F8 (`ui.symbolOptions`) opens Symbol Options for a new symbol made from the selection, as the Symbols panel's New Symbol button does. Not yet diffed item by item
against Illustrator's published default shortcut list; estimated ~70% of it. Remaining: the panel shortcuts,
the type shortcuts (tracking/kerning/leading/baseline steps with Alt/Shift), and selection shortcuts in the
Layers panel. The shortcut editor also lists the commands that are in no menu but run without parameters (Edit › Duplicate,
panel-menu commands such as Break Link to Symbol), so they can be given shortcuts (#1539). It lists each button of the
Align panel's Align Objects, Distribute Objects and Distribute Spacing rows under Align, with no default shortcut, and a
shortcut set there runs what a click on the button runs, with the panel's Align To choice and Distribute Spacing value. The [ and ] keys reach the Blob Brush, the Eraser and the Symbolism tools in the app (#1734, part of #1661).

Cmd/Ctrl+Alt+N (`file.newWithoutDialog`, New Without Dialog in the shortcut editor) makes a document with the
settings the New Document dialog opens on (the first Recent entry, or the Print category's Letter while Recent is
empty), without opening the dialog.

## Look and feel

- [x] Recolor Artwork › Assign: double-click a row's new color to pick it in the Color Picker, in the model it was picked in (part of #4028); a Lab new color is kept but can't be typed yet (the picker's Lab fields work in RGB); Lab entry, swatch libraries and the row menu remain.

- [x] Recolor Artwork Edit color chips scroll within the screen, preserving the wheel and actions (#3416); 5, 20, 200 and 400 colors, resizing and UI scaling, final-chip editing and preview/history covered by egui_kittest.

- [x] Status messages and bounded readouts remain stable as values, canvas hover and background jobs change; ordinary values fit all registered languages and units, and optional groups yield in narrow windows (regressions; contributor rebuilt and confirmed the original-file status-bar scenario on 2026-10-19). Sample widths are cached per UI and refreshed only when language, document units or pixels per point changes.

- [x] Cached canvas dimensions match the renderer limit before cache-key creation, view construction and worker submission (controlled-worker regression).
- [x] Cached canvas frames keep their document scale during panel/window resize, including rotated views and DPI changes (controlled-worker regression; contributor rebuilt and confirmed the original-file resize scenario on 2026-10-19).
Medium Dark theme and four brightness levels, the categorized and Advanced toolbars, 35 pt document tabs,
33 pt panel tabs, hint bar, contextual task bar and native macOS menus, restyled to values measured from
public screenshots on 2026-10-02 (~75–80% then). Missing: a System theme that follows the OS (#825), a
re-measure of the panels added since. Slider rails show on Medium Dark (#1808, #1662).

- [x] Native saves restore Show Transparency Grid (#1968), without dirtying the document or adding an undo step.

- [x] Compact Color Picker with a distinct header, stacked actions and aligned input groups. #2008; translated action widths, visible warnings, stable mode switching and vertical scrolling in narrow windows; real egui events and two UI scales covered; German and enlarged English renders reviewed locally. Scores unchanged

## Highlighted-text Eyedropper (2026-10-21)

Type/Eyedropper switches preserve highlighted characters; sampling changes only their enabled
font/paint attributes. Enabled non-neutral source or target object transparency is refused
atomically; disable Transparency to preserve object transparency while sampling the range.
`tests/eyedropper.rs` covers ranges, Unicode, history and transparency; tool tests cover brush-key
sampling coexistence. Scores and estimates unchanged. See [development.md](development.md#highlighted-text-eyedropper-2026-10-21).

## Drag artwork between document tabs (2026-10-22)

Selection drags activate another visible document tab after 400 ms, then copy editable artwork
on canvas release with the original grab point. Source and clipboard preservation, resource
rendering, swatch conflicts, cancellation, rotated/zoomed views and target Undo/Redo have regression
coverage (`tests_document_drag`, `tests_clipboard`). Overflow-menu and cross-window drag remain
separate. Scores and hours unchanged. [Behavior and command details](development.md#drag-artwork-between-document-tabs-2026-10-22).

In isolation mode Direct Selection and Group Selection pick only inside the isolated object (#1666); Escape cancels a Direct Selection drag (#1803, #1743); Escape while typing ends the edit, keeps the type selected and switches to the Selection tool (#1802, #1686, #1656); A filled Pencil path keeps a stroke of None (#1804, part of #1679); With the Pen, a press that moves under 3 screen pixels is a click (a corner without handles), and a Precise Cursors command toggles the existing preference and can take a shortcut (#1663); The [ and ] keys reach the Blob Brush, the Eraser and the Symbolism tools in the app (#1734, part of #1661); In the Pathfinder panel a group selected alone counts its nested shapes, so an expanded stroked object keeps the Shape Modes enabled (#1642, #1635); Object › Expand turns radial, grid and mirror repeats into groups of their instances (#1765, #1756); Select › Same matches type on its characters' fill and stroke (#1746, #1737); Fields commit what was typed when you click away or move to another field, as Enter does; Escape still discards it (#1787); Up and Down move through the command search results and Enter runs the highlighted one (#1798, #1757); Slider rails show on Medium Dark (#1808, #1662); On Windows the custom title bar's window stays truly borderless, so the pointer lines up with what is drawn on systems where the hidden caption offset it (#1791, #1758; awaiting the reporter's confirmation); Tool cursors are painted into the window on Wayland (#1652; not run on Linux, #1645 stays open until confirmed); the guide-position row (#1659) is now "not scored": the documentation found doesn't establish it, and it is kept as a convenience pending the owner's decision; scores unchanged |
